All he could do was cast the [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) and pass the turn back, looking mournfully at his [Stoneforge Mystic](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stoneforge+Mystic) in the graveyard. Randle continued to ramp up his mana and, with the help of his [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), cast a turn 4 [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) to fetch up a [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) and a [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge). James untapped, cast his [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf), cascaded into a Fauna shaman and quickly conceded.

James Cleak 1 - 1 Jonathan Randle

As they shuffled, James and Jonathan chatted briefly about the swiss section of the tournament. James said that he had lost round 1 but it was all part of a master plan to dodge the top decks and players and sneak into the top 8. Seemed that plan worked well!

**Game 3**

The recurring theme for the match was continued with both players playing turn 1 mana producers. Both also had a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to follow. James opted to use his [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to fetch [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) and shot down Randle's [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). Jonathan used his to fetch up [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence). James added a [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) and a second [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to the board while Randle simply cast the angel before passing the turn. James untapped and looked unhappily at his own Linnvala in hand but, with the [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) now inactive, only one white source available meant he was unable to destroy Randle's copy of the legendary creature. Instead he cast a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and crashed in with all of his creatures, hoping to overwhelm Randle who dropped to 6 from the attack. This plan was quickly put on hold by the Jonathan's second angel, this time a Baneslayer.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ca/fa/cafa12344eae2bd9dc714311354f20d7e9d830bad8ade7b20b54f2bfbb9b4816.jpg)*James Cleak works it out*

Draw after draw failed to yield the second white source for James Cleak, but he was able to add two [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) to his side, 4/4s due to [Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) and [Arid Mesa](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Arid+Mesa). Jonathan simply matched him knight for knight, gumming up the board while the Baneslayer flew overhead recovering the life lost from James' attacks. Four turns later, its job was done.

James Cleak 1 - 2 Jonathan Randle

It seems appropriate that Linvala is such an important card in this match, given that she is the Keeper of [Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Silence). There has been very little chat in so far. In fact, nobody is saying a bloody word

**Game 4**

This game saw the second mulligan of the match for Jonathan Randle who seemed much happier with his six. This time James had the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) but Jonathan was only able to put a [Sunpetal Grove](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sunpetal+Grove) into play tapped. James had a [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine) as his second land drop but nothing to spend his two available mana on. Jonathan had a [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) and then attempted to lay another [Sunpetal Grove](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sunpetal+Grove) and [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) before his opponent quickly pointed out that you are only allowed to lay one land a turn in **Magic** the Gathering, prompting a swift warning for violation of game state and a blushing Jonathan Randle. Sometimes, you can be so focused on future plans that you forget the present ones!

James had a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) ready to smash in the next turn and his [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine) joined it the turn after dropping Randle to a lowly 8. Jonathan just continued to build up his base with a couple of [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch)s and a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) dealt with the [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine) but James just threw another [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) into the fray forcing Randle to throw a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) shaped speed bump into the way in order to survive, its parting gift to search up a [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel). But Jonathan didn't have the fifth land for the Baneslayer and could only manage a Linvala, Keeper of SIlence. [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) for James found a [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) and the resulting attack dropped Randle to 1 with Linvala trading for a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) but the other [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) taking down [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). Again Randle missed his fifth land but this time had two more offerings in the shape of [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) and [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) number three to survive at 1 for another turn. Cleak had a [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine) to replace the Bloodbraid but had drawn only lands so was unable to add anything further to his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) on board.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/8f/a6/8fa698ca4d9a162f212e6f325921b0d1f699f5b5f608b0c47ba1d9667c625bcf.jpg)*Jonathan Randle vs James Cleak*

Randle untapped and found his land for Baneslayer, halting the attacks for a turn and then added another [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence), at least temporarily preventing any potential lethal [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) draws. James just continued to draw land but one of them was a second [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine). Linvala took up offensive duties but James found another [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and sent it crashing in along with its brother and one of the Ravines. Baneslayer took down the [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine) but one of the [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch)s went to Randle's graveyard and even with Baneslayer's life gain, he dropped back down to 2. Linvala continued to attack but the army of [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch)s were shrinking, reducing the effect of exalted, but James was still down to 10.

Randle stabilised his board a little with a [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) while all James could manage was a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). James found a [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) which would have forced his Raging Raving through for lethal damage but for the [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) that Randle had drawn the turn before! The active [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) meant that James didn't even get any life from his [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar), with the [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) gaining protection from red from Randle's own [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe). Jonathan decided it was time for [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) enter the red zone, giving him a comfortable life cushion and dropping James to 5. James tried another attack with the now 6/6 [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine), again with [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) equipped but a second [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) search prevented any life gain. Cleak took a glance at his hand, a look at the board and offered the handshake.

**James Cleak 1 - 3 Jonathan Randle**

  

## Semifinals – Joe Jackson v Jonathan Randle

*by Rich Hagon*

With Andrew Morrison vanquished in the quarter finals, Joe Jackson now carries the remaining hopes of a first GB Champion from Scotland. His opponent, Jonathan Randle, is here at this stage for the second time in three years, having lifted the title in 2008 and led the team at Worlds in Memphis that year.

With both players starting at six cards, Randle was quicker out of the blocks, with [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) leading to [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) on turn two. Jackson's [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) played fair, arriving on turn three. Randle made a second Knight on his turn three, while Jackson went to work with [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel).

Three Knights of the Reliquary on the battlefield meant the graveyards would be packed with land pretty quickly. Randle added [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), the Haste irrelevant. In came the Baneslayer, before Jackson matched [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) for [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). When Randle attacked with one of his Knights, Jackson fetched up [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) to protect his own [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), while Randle pulled the same well-known stunt to keep his own Knight on the battlefield. A second [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) completed the turn for the former champion.

[Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) Cascaded into [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) for Jackson, who reached for the skies once again with his [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel). He now led by twenty three life to eight. Randle was in no imminent danger of dying, however, since he had two [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) and a [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) for the Baneslayer to slog through before death would become a real possibility. [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) allowed Jackson to shoot down one of the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) before sending the Baneslayer once again.

Randle had nothing, and that was the cue for Jackson to start abusing [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). He discarded [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) to fetch [Stoneforge Mystic](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stoneforge+Mystic), which in turn would complete the [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) / [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) combo. It was a pretty collection of creatures assembled on Jackson's side of the battlefield: [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), Baneslayer Angle, [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf), [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), [Stoneforge Mystic](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stoneforge+Mystic), [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage).

Randle activated his [Stirring Wildwood](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stirring+Wildwood), knowing that it was destined for Deathtouch death at the hands of the [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage), meaning he was down to just three. He continued to thin his library with [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), but found no help.

Jackson 1 – 0 Randle.

![Fauna Shaman](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Fauna+Shaman)

[Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) opened unopposed for Randle, who discarded [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) on turn three to run out [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), and the returning [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), which promptly smashed face. Three mana for Jackson meant [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage), quickly shooting a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), but Randle was chock full of answers, adding [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence). It was ironic that [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) for Jackson Cascaded into a second [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage), just as functionally useless as the first. Moments later, Randle had made the best of five into the best of three.

Jackson 1 – 1 Randle.

Randle again had acceleration early in game three. While Jackson opened on turn two [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage), Randle had [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) turn one, and both [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) and [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) turn two. In a repeat of game two, Jackson used three mana for [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage), and immediately killed the [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch). Randle still had more than enough for [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), enough to leave [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) activation.

Jackson, meanwhile, had assembled the [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) to go with his [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage). Randle was down to twelve, as he activated [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), discarding [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) in favor of [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence). Unsurprisingly, that caused a grunt of irritation from Jackson.

A fresh [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) from the Scot dropped Randle to seven, with [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) providing Exalted support. Thanks to [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), Randle had lifegain at the ready, searching up [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) and sending it onto the battlefield. Jackson, though, had a SPECTACULAR draw, finding [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) on top of his deck. That sent away Linvala, and allowed [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) to resume utterly obscene beatings. In moments, the game was effectively done, and Jackson stood on the verge of the championship match.

Jackson 2 – 1 Randle.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/e6/dc/e6dcf7144a050e8f34e32a165089fe226ffa51b72f33349905a66ed5be69dc89.jpg)*Joe Jackson - we have it on good authority that he is wearing shoes.*

As we waited for game four to begin, Scottish spectator Silas Bath jokingly reminisced, 'I remember when you used to come into the store and get battered at Draft. 0-3 Jackson, we used to call you.' And another watching wag replied, 'And that was just last week!'

Onto game four, and time to get serious again, not least because the loser of this one would face a titanic struggle in the 3rd/4th playoff for a slot on the GB Team against either Dan Gardner or Richard Bland, both fearsome opponents, and on top of their game, judging by the appreciate crowd watching the second semi final.

Needing a win, Randle mulliganed to six, but still began with turn one [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), a move matched by Jackson. Turn two [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) was also the play for both players. The symmetry shifted when Randle laid a land and passed, while Jackson exploded into [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel). Never one for open displays of emotion at the table, Randle let a small exhalation escape his lips. To seasoned Randle-watchers, that was a big deal.

Jackson landed [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage), and attempted to end the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) Randle had laid the previous turn. [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) for [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) prevented that plan for at least one turn, but Jackson was twenty-five to eleven ahead, and clearly growing in confidence with every turn. Randle ran his hand through his hair, deep in the tank, and deep in trouble. [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence) would keep [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) in check, at least for now.

Looking at Jackson's hand, it was hard to imagine what Randle might have to get him back in the match. Jackson attacked with [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), with Randle activating [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) for a second [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe). Jackson cast [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring), but Randle had named white with [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe), and Jackson couldn't target Linvala. He disposed of [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), but that wasn't the plan at all. [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) added to a battlefield that should have had 'game over' written all over it.

It was a massive moment, but how massive could Randle make it?

Next turn, he cast [Sovereigns of Lost Alara](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sovereigns+of+Lost+Alara), fetched up [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription), equipped Linvala, and piled in for more damage than a force nine gale. Despite this, the scores remained favorable for Jackson by eleven to eight. The Scot tapped four mana for [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), and sent in the team.

Ever the gentleman, Randle extended the hand, and Scotland's Joe Jackson was through to the championship match.

Joe Jackson 3 – 1 Jonathan Randle.

  

## Semifinals – Dan Gardner vs Richard Bland

*by Tim Willoughby*

As Richard Bland and Dan Gardner shuffled up for their best of five semi-finals, they each postured on how little testing they had done with their decks.

"Joe and I built our deck the night before the tournament"

"I got a list of Quentin and just went with that"

"Fine, but I haven't tested this matchup, I only tested for the quarter-finals."

"I just drafted last night…"

It's good to know that once you are talking about the semi-finals for the national championships, that everyone is well prepared.

Of course, in their own way, these players were both well prepared. They've both been playing great ***Magic*** in the last year. Richard got to level 3 last round in the Pro Players Club, while Dan hit level 4 (assuming he goes to Worlds). For these players, the points would be valuable in securing more solid position on the Pro Tour.

Dan won the roll, and each player took a mulligan. Bland then took a second. While Richard did have a [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) for the second turn, he didn't have a third land, and was without a green source. Meanwhile, Dan had a turn four [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor) who was soon put to work fatesealing Bland's deck. It looked like the first game of the match would be a quick one.

Dan cast a [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), to which Bland just smiled and scooped.

"That was a pretty nice keep. Five lands and Jace."

Dan Gardner 1 – 0 Richard Bland

![](https://i.ormos.uk/b1/8c/b18c87b4a4ba4501100e1f2eac48fa72053c5c21e0c6510506ea8b6a4d09eeef.jpg)*Bland - is this hand a keeper.*

For the second game, Bland kept a full seven, and had a turn two [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) off [Stirring Wildwood](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stirring+Wildwood), before playing a [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) that hit [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). Bland snuck a [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) into play with is remaining mana. That collar would be good in the face of [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens), that came from Gardner, and if the [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) from Bland got a chance to hang around, it would pose real problems for the reigning National Champion.

Gardner cast [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor) and used the planeswalker to bounce Fauna Shama. This tapped him out, leaving Bland free to resolve something. That something was a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), which ran straight at Jace, only to be chump blocked by [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens).

Gardner was up to five mana, and used that mana to cast [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), while Jace bounced the [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). Bland calmly used [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) to deal with [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), and attacked Jace with his [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) to finish the planeswalker off.

Dan cast [Sphinx of Jwar Isle](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sphinx+of+Jwar+Isle), that in the face of [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) with exalted was not even a great blocker. Bland attacked Dan down to 11. With the Sphinx around, Dan always knew the top card of his deck, and he liked the card on top enough to draw it with a [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens). Sphinx then cracked in before Dan reduced Richard to just 3 lands thanks to [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge).

Richard played a new fourth land, and recast his [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). This was hit by [Cancel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cancel), tapping Dan out, and allowing Richard to equip his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) unimpeded, letting his attacks turn the life totals around to 11 – 17 in Bland's favour. Dan cast [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor) and had a think.

"You should play a deck like mine… it's far easier"

![Vengevine](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Vengevine)

Dan smiled and bounced [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). Dan was tapped out of blue mana, so Richard was fine to resolve his spells, and started with [Sylvan Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sylvan+Ranger) to find a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest). From there came [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), that went for Jace, but in fact just killed a [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens).

Dan attacked with [Sphinx of Jwar Isle](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sphinx+of+Jwar+Isle) and [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade). He then bounced [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) yet again, stunting the amount that Richard could get done on his turn. Bland was on just 3 from attacks, to Dan's 11, so he really needed to achieve quite a lot for the turn in order to stay in the game.

Bland chose not to play [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), instead casting [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) and hitting a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) that would serve as a crucial blocker in the face of Gardner's flyers. Without a clear win available, Dan cast [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity) and passed. Insodoing, he again tapped out of blue mana, and didn't draw into any more to even represent a counterspell.

Gardner cursed Bland's luck in hitting the [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) on the cascade. It had bought him time, allowing [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) to put him back in the game. Swings from [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) made the life totals 10 to 4 in Bland's favour. Suddenly the game could be anyone's. [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) from Bland threatened danger the very next turn, as it could fetch up a [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) to make his [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) unblockable by the lone [Sphinx of Jwar Isle](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sphinx+of+Jwar+Isle) on Gardner's side of the board.

Gardner cast [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) and passed. After a little thought, Bland cast [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). He used his [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) to get that Steppe and give [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) protection from white, after having equipped it with [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar). Bland was now up to 15, and with Gardner having used a fetchland to go to 3, it meant ha had to be a little cagey. Knowing that another Steppe could be coming, Dan attacked with Baneslayer to go up to 8, and kill of [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). He then played a second Baneslayer.

Bland equipped and attacked with an 9/9 [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) thanks to exalted. When [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) blocked, each player ended up on a much healthier life total, even if Baneslayer herself took something of a dip in health. A [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) from Bland was stopped by [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze), and once again Gardner was faced with touch decisions to stay alive. Attacks from [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) made the life totals 19 – 18 to Dan. Did nobody tell these players the directions life totals were meant to go in? Dan followed up with Elspeth, [Knight Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+Errant), which made a token. Another [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) stopped [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage), that would otherwise threaten Elspeth. When [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) was cast, Bland got back a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), to allow for some big swings. Elspeth lost one loyalty, while Dan went to 7 on the combat. Gardner's defence was bolstered by a [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens), and he had a [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) to draw with, before using Elspeth to pump [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) for the attacks.

Bland split his attacks between both planeswalkers and Dan himself. [Sylvan Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sylvan+Ranger) and [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) had a go at Elspeth, [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) and [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) went in on Jace, while [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) went right for Dan. [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) blocked [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) to keep Elspeth around, but Jace was done for, and Dan himself dropped quite a bit lower on the swings. Gardner was still alive though, and with a topdecked [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile) he was able to deal with [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), and re-establish some sort of uneasy control of the game.

Elspeth letting [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) attack for 8 and [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) were between them causing the game to run long. Bland attacked Elspeth with more or less his whole team in order that Bland might be able to be the only one gaining life at any quick pace. In the face of this assault, Dan decided to change tack. He used Elspeth to pump [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) and ran in with that instead.

Bland did what he had been doing for much of the game, casting creatures, getting back [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), and making big attacks. As he had cast a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) as one of his creatures, that [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) got to kill Elspeth in one, being too big for [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) to block. Dan cast an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring), having a long think about what to remove, as there was an active [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) on the other side of the board. He eventually took [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) into exile with the enchantment, before swinging in with his angel.

[Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) got back that collar, and it got equipped right back to [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), which swung in alongside [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine), [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf), [Sylvan Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sylvan+Ranger) and [Stoneforge Mystic](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Stoneforge+Mystic). [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) and [Celestial Purge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Purge) dealt with Ravine and [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) respectively, and the [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) that was the last card in Gardner's hand stopped a [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary). Gardner dropped to 11, while Bland went up to 14.

Gardner almost fell out of his chair when another [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) came down for Bland. He had held back his [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) in the hope that it could hold off [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), but the extra point of exalted meant that this was just not an option. It seemed that Gardner was out of cards in hand and out of luck.

"I'm such a lucksack" remarked Bland, enjoying the chance to turn the screws a little after how game one had gone.

Eventually [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) was relegated to chump blocking, and with Gardner on just 9, he needed to draw something special fast. That something special was [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile), which would mean Bland would have to turn to another creature to do his attacking for subsequent turns. Fortunately for him, he had plenty.

A [Sun Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sun+Titan) from Bland fetched back [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary). It seemed a mere attack step away from the match being levelled, but neither player was in any great hurry. It was not until [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) fetched a [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) to take out the remaining blocker that Gardner had that he finally scooped up his cards.

Dan Gardner 1 – 1 Richard Bland

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ec/3b/ec3b0d19889978e42efe0becf16a60061dfa1836622100b63638365f44112628.jpg)*Gardner in the zone.*

Gardner looked at the top card of his deck in disgust. It was the [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) that might have put him back in things. Too late. Too bad.

Both players kept for game three, and it was Bland with the first play in [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). Turn two saw a [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage), and an attack for 1 from those very birds. Bland was taking it slow to deploy threats in the face of untapped lands from Gardner. He merrily got his beat on with his pridemage without casting a spell on turn three, and got in for four with it the following turn when [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) hit the board.

Eventually, Gardner could wait no longer, and pulled the trigger on the [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile) that he might have hoped to use on something a little more exciting. A [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) from Bland was a nice test spell, and it resolved before another [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) let the first attack for two.

Dan played his second spell of the game, in [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity), digging for answers. He found a [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile) for [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), and a [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) to make the red zone a much less friendly place. With Gardner tapped out, Bland took the opportunity to resolve a [Realm Razer](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Realm+Razer), and followed up with [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) off his mana creatures. Control plans no longer seemed an option for Gardner.

With all his mana creatures, Bland was operating just fine without much land. He used [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) to fetch [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) and cast it. His attacks put Dan on 8. Dan was now holding back [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) on defence. He had got up to two lands, but his board was dwarfed by that of Bland. Richard next cast a [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary). Already a 6/6, the knight would be an 8/8 if it attacke alone, and that is precisely how much life Dan had.

Richard naturally drew the [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) that would allow him to attack unimpeded, and it was on to game three.

Dan Gardner 1 – 2 Richard Bland

After two rough games, Gardner seemed rattled. If this had been best of three it would all be over. He still had a chance at repeating his victory in 2009, but it would require two wins on the trot in this match.

On the play, Dan had a mulligan, but was ready with a [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) to stop the first play of Bland, a [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage). A second Pridemage did make it in, but was unable to attack effectively, as Elspeth came out to create soldiers. The Pridemage did go for Elspeth, backed up by a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), but could not get close to killing her.

When Dan played a [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), he must have felt good about his position. A quick [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe) allowed [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) to off Elspeth though, and an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) meant that [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) was out of the game. Once more Bland also had [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar), to make racing much harder.

Suddenly on the back foot, Dan used a fetchland to get up to 6 mana to cast [Sphinx of Jwar Isle](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sphinx+of+Jwar+Isle). The Sphinx didn't block [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), and Dan could only look on as first [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), and then [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) joined Richard's side of the board. An [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) on [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) meant that at least it wouldn't be active in the short term, though [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage) could get it back. [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge)s also gradually reduced the mana available to Bland to do things. There was enough mana to equip [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and make some swings to take the life totals to 12 – 17 in Bland's favour. At the end of Dan's turn, which passed with just a Sphinx swing, Bland got back his [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) from under [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) with that Pridemage.

Attacks from Bland put Gardner at 7. His [Sphinx of Jwar Isle](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sphinx+of+Jwar+Isle) was doing a fine job of attacking in, but judging by the scowl on Gardner's face, he would rather not have known how the top of his deck looked. There was another [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) there though, which locked down [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) again.

Dan could only look on, head in hands, as [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) edged Bland further and further ahead. He used it first to find a [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), then to discard that [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) to find [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf). Seven power of haste was enough to finish the 2009 champion, leaving Richard Bland the victor.

Richard Bland wins 3 – 1

  

## 3rd / 4th playoff - Jonathan Randle v Dan Gardner

*by Rich Hagon*

It's one of the biggest matches of the ***Magic*** year – the battle for third at Nationals. Rarely has Great Britain seen a higher quality pair competing for the final slot than 2008 Champion Jonathan Randle, and defending 2009 Champion Dan Gardner. It's a fitting end to another superb weekend for both of them. While both will play with honor at Worlds in Chiba, only one can contribute to a team performance that will be paced by finalists Joe Jackson of Scotland and England's Richard Bland.

[Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) got Randle off to a quick start, with Gardner gaining the first of what he hoped would be huge card advantage via [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens). He added a second Wall, but not before Randle had landed [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) unmolested by counter***Magic***. That [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) soon began [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) shenanigans for Randle, with Gardner trying to get his head in the game after a brain-pounding semi final against Richard Bland.

Gardner found [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile) for the [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), with Randle responding, discarding a second [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) and tutoring up a replacement [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). Four more mana tapped Gardner out, but brought him [Elspeth, Knight-Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elspeth%2C+Knight-Errant), plus a Soldier token. The token died when Randle brought back his [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s, Gardner choosing to keep both [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) intact. [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) drew the control man a card, and it was back to Randle, looking to find a way through.

The [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine)s continued to eat away the following turn, with [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) and Soldier token hitting the graveyard. When Gardner attempted [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), Randle turned control mage, with [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) threatening the plan. Gardner had the answer answered with [Negate](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Negate), and the Baneslayer hit the battlefield running. Flying. Whatever.

Randle continued to make use of his second [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) of the game, before casting [Sovereigns of Lost Alara](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sovereigns+of+Lost+Alara), equipping [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) with [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription), and turning sideways in emphatic fashion. With double [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and the [Sovereigns of Lost Alara](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sovereigns+of+Lost+Alara), Randle's [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) was a colossal 17/16. With Trample. [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) blocked, but it was all academic, as Gardner drew no answers, giving the lead to Randle.

Randle 1 – 0 Gardner.

It seemed as if luck was deserting Gardner. He mulliganed to start game two, and found Randle with an explosive opening of [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) into [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) completed turn two. At least Gardner had [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) for the tutoring machine. In came the Cobra, but Randle had nothing to press home his advantage. [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) was next for Gardner, netting him a card, and effectively gaining him two life, as [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) ended the blue Planeswalker.

[Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) looked to shut up shop, but a second [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) for Randle meant [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) now had four power, enough to take down the [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) should Gardner block. He didn't, and fell to thirteen. It was clear that he was happy enough to let [Jace Beleren](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace+Beleren) die, because his next turn brought the upgrade – [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor), which bounced Randle's Hierarch, keeping him off unfair amounts of mana.

Randle was really stuggling for land, getting no benefit from the landfall ability on [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra), and Gardner used his remaining mana in Randle's upkeep to off the Cobra permanently, continuing the strategy begun with Jace. Randle played another [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra), and again Gardner was happy to exchange a loyalty counter for the Cobra back in hand. He added [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) to his growing collection of permanents, and was starting to look in good shape.

That said, Randle had a full grip of seven cards, and he put two of them to good use the following turn when giving [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) protection with [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe), and finishing off [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor). Gardner added a second [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), attacked with the first, and left himself tapped out once more. Finally reaching six mana, Randle gave the crowd a glimpse of orange, as M11 Mythic [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) hit the battlefield.

In came Gardner for ten flying damage, leaving the scores twenty eight to four in his favor. Randle began his turn with [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra), then attacking with [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) and [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). Two fetch lands arriving tapped still got him mana via the Cobra, and he cast [Summoning Trap](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Summoning+Trap), finding himself a [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel). Randle ended the turn with [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), but when Gardner drew three cards with [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity), he drew into [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring), meaning the pair of Baneslayers could even the match at one each.

Randle 1 – 1 Gardner.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/68/62/6862f92ebebd1ce9d2b32e7a41bff423fe72ab85e0cd78c9cc501165dfbba0bc.jpg)*Randle playing for a shot on the team.*

Nothing happened until turn four in the third game, with Randle's [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) quickly disappearing via [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile). That had to be good news for Gardner, as did having a second [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile) for [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) when Randle attempted to deal the first damage of the game with the 4/4 Vigilant flyer. More good Gardner news – three new cards via [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity), as threats continued to be non-existent. It kept on getting better. Two [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) meant two more cards. Shields up. Plenty of mana. A ton of cards. Did I mention things were going well for Gardner?

Randle had [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) for approximately three seconds before Gardner cast [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile). Please see the previous sentence. Gardner added [Elspeth, Knight-Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elspeth%2C+Knight-Errant) to the board. Ditto. Finally, Randle did something that might (or might not) have an impact on the game – [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan). It met [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile). Etc. Etc. Etc.

So, I've mentioned fifty three good things for Gardner. Time for one bad one – [Eldrazi Conscription](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Eldrazi+Conscription) on [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), that resolved despite [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity) drawing Gardner three more cards. Eek. Gardner hit back with [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor) bouncing the Hierarch, killing the Conscription in the process, but Randle went back to work with [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel). Away went Jace, which Gardner objected to, so much so that he cast [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) for the [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel), getting his Planeswalker back. Surely now that little Randle fightback was no more than a hiccup?

Not so. Randle now had enough mana to activate both his [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade)s, and powered into action. Jace bit the dust, replaced by another from Gardner, but the reigning champion was clearly feeling the strain of his earlier exertions. Randle cast [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), and then went for [Summoning Trap](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Summoning+Trap), meeting [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). Double [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) from Gardner sent both Colonnades to the graveyard, while [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) showed why Gardner had allowed the [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman).

[Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) was next for Gardner, and he allowed [Summoning Trap](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Summoning+Trap) to resolve for Randle, who got [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) for his troubles. [Wall of Reverence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Reverence) joined the team, and with [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) an 8/8, Randle moved at a stroke from twelve life back to twenty. Gardner used his final [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) on the Knight, and sent [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) to steal a quarter of Randle's life. At 2/2, Randle's new [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) wasn't such a lifegain beating as the Knight had been. Gardner shortened the clock with a second [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel).

Randle activated [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), getting him [War Priest of Thune](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=War+Priest+of+Thune). So far, so good. But when he cast it to end Gardner's [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) on his [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel), he found a nasty surprise waiting for him – [Gather Specimens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gather+Specimens)! Sorry, that should be a bit more dramatic...

[GATHER SPECIMENS](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=GATHER+SPECIMENS).

![Gather Specimens](http://gatherer.wizards.com/Handlers/Image.ashx?type=card&name=Gather+Specimens)

When [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel) tried to re-enter the battlefield, it did so on Gardner's side of the field. Unreal.

See? Told you it was all going well for Gardner.

Randle 1 – 2 Gardner.
