Since Randle had lost the previous game when he'd first done something meaningful on turn eleventy-nine, a turn one [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) was a welcome turn one play. He couldn't capitalize on turn two, but added both land and [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) turn three. [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) met [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile), with Gardner offering [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor). With his control opponent tapped out, Randle had a turn to make use of a maximum seven mana. His choice was to activate [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade), and destroy the Planeswalker. Now untapped, Gardner passed the turn.

Randle's turn produced [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) for him, and [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity) for Gardner. [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) dealt with the Knight, and it was back to Randle. [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan)? [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak) right back atcha. [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) resolved for Randle, causing a large sigh from Gardner. Still, [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) wasn't shabby.

Randle rebuilt with [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) and [War Priest of Thune](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=War+Priest+of+Thune), while Gardner started again with [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel). [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra) didn't seem like it changed much. In came the Baneslayer, but Randle had an even bigger flyer, [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel). [Elspeth, Knight-Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elspeth%2C+Knight-Errant) came next for Gardner, Randle sitting pretty with his [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel) eating up Gardner's Baneslayer.

Then came a huge moment. [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) looked to deal with [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel). Randle sacrificed his [Verdant Catacombs](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Verdant+Catacombs), triggering Landfall, which meant his Angel could [Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Exile)[War Priest of Thune](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=War+Priest+of+Thune). When the [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) resolved, the [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel) went away, but [War Priest of Thune](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=War+Priest+of+Thune) came back, and so did the Angel!

Carnage. Utter, utter carnage. Still, at least Gardner got his [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) back. The words 'scant consolation' spring to mind. Randle had [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan), and that meant major [Admonition Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Admonition+Angel) action, sending both Baneslayer and Soldier token away. In came Randle's Angel, and the following turn he activated [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) before sending in a veritable mountain of damage, including [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) to rob Gardner of even [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) as a potential blocker. Randle added [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), and Gardner swept them up.

Randle 2 – 2 Gardner.

![](https://i.ormos.uk/2b/6f/2b6fed575d62d3ff5a138e3feb5064a8613ed9d848849cab7fca94d258bc2a78.jpg)*The Constant Gardner.*

Now this is where ***Magic*** gets cruel. More than six hours after these two started the top 8 this morning, one single duel separates them from the finish line, and membership of the team of three for Worlds 2010. Both have demonstrated incredible fortitude in the past two years, and now again. Both have fallen just short of the dizzying heights of raising the trophy, but this is still possibly the most important single game of ***Magic*** either has ever played.

Showtime.

Turn two [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) for Randle met [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile), Gardner having established early defense with [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens). Turn three meant [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) for Randle, with Gardner taking the five points. A second [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) drew Gardner a card, before doing the perfect job of blocking [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine). Randle passed, but so did Gardner, sitting on four land. [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) ate the second Wall, with Gardner aiming [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile) in order to find land number five.

Knowing that Gardner had to deal with [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), Randle sat with a wall of cards in hand, sending the hasty green man in for five more damage. [Jace's Ingenuity](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%27s+Ingenuity) at end of turn met [Mana Leak](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mana+Leak), leaving Gardner on the brink. He cast [Jace, the Mind Sculptor](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Jace%2C+the+Mind+Sculptor), and went into [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) mode. Back we went, [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine) apparently unopposed, and with six mana available for Randle to work with. In came [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), and Gardner fell to five. Out came [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan). It was all one way traffic in the decider. Did Gardner have one more trick up his sleeve?

[Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) via Planeswalker began the next turn for Gardner. It could well be his last. [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment) swept the board. Back to Randle. Primeveal Titan met [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze). [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) triggered [Vengevine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Vengevine), but Gardner had [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile)! Still alive....barely.

More [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm), facing [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and a pair of [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade)s, one of which Randle could activate the following turn. [Elspeth, Knight-Errant](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Elspeth%2C+Knight-Errant) made a Soldier, and Gardner passed. Was THIS the turn that Randle claimed third place on the national team?

[Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman) resolved. [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) activated, [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) killed it. What a game.

[Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) for Gardner yet again. [Day of Judgment](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Day+of+Judgment). Make a Soldier. Pass the turn.

Gardner had [Flashfreeze](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Flashfreeze) for [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), leaving Randle to cast [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch). In theory, Randle's remaining [Celestial Colonnade](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Celestial+Colonnade) would now be lethal. [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) via Jace suggested Gardner already had an answer, and with a second [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge), he did. Elspeth went to seven, a second Soldier hit the battlefield. Was Gardner going to pull this one out of the bag?

The Colonnade attacked, [Tectonic Edge](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tectonic+Edge) killed it. Randle cast [War Priest of Thune](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=War+Priest+of+Thune). Gardner made Soldier number three, Elspeth went to eight, Jace continued to [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm), [Wall of Omens](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Wall+of+Omens) drew Gardner a card. [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) joined Randle's team, along with [Lotus Cobra](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Cobra). It wasn't going to be enough. [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) met [Path to Exile](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Path+to+Exile), with Soldier number four arriving.

[Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel) for Gardner. Eight counters off Elspeth for Gardner. Everything Indestructible for Gardner. [Cancel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cancel) for [Primeval Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Primeval+Titan) for Gardner. An 8/8 Baneslayer thanks to Elspeth crashed in. Gardner added a second Baneslayer, bounced Randle's [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise), and swung for the fences.

An incredible end to an incredible weekend.

Jonathan Randle 2 – 3 Dan Gardner.

  

## Finals – Joe Jackson vs Richard Bland

*by Tim Willoughby*

As the finals began, Dan Gardner leaned over from the third/fourth place playoff.

"Take a really long time playing so that I can stay champion for a little longer!"

This best-of-five match would see these two friends who play together regularly at Warwick University in a 75 card mirror match, and it could easily take a while. Both players worked together on their deck the very night before the tournament, and between them it has served them very well indeed. While they are both currently based around Warwick, Joe is originally Scottish, and against the English Richard Bland, he is vying to become the first Scottish champion of Great Britain.

The mirror match die roll saw a couple of draws, and then both players took a mulligan to 6. Richard, who had won the roll, then went down to 5. Then 4.

"I'm in your head!" declared Joe triumphantly.

"When I lose, I lose in style" retorted Bland.

"I'll keep!"

"Is it good?"

Bland just laughed, played a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and passed the turn. He let out a little groan when he saw a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) from Jackson. There was no play from Bland on turn two, while Jacskon had a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman).

The only way that Bland was in the race was from fetchland damage that Joe is taking. This didn't really help all that much in the long run though, as [Cunning Sparkmage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cunning+Sparkmage) joined team Scotland.

By the time that Bland had a second land, Joe also had a [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence). While Bland's second land did allow a [Sylvan Ranger](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sylvan+Ranger) that found land number 3, it all seemed immaterial as Joe was merrily going off, fetching and casting a [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) to get another [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman).

"I didn't get there on the mull to four. Surprisingly." Bland scooped up his cards. It was on to Game 2.

Joe Jackson 1 – 0 Richard Bland

"I don't really mind having all my bad luck concentrated into one game" remarked Bland casually as he worked out his sideboarding. He had lost Game 1 of his semi-finals to Dan Gardner in similar fashion.

Richard, on the play, again took a mulligan, while Joe was happy on his seven. Again Joe had a turn one [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch), while Bland's first play of the game was a [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman). That accelerant from Joe was enough to ensure that on turn two he could cast an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) and keep the Shaman from going too crazy. Jackson had a Shaman of his own, which prompted an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring) from Richard. Mirror matches eh? Don't you just love them?

A [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) was the next play for Jackson, who even got to beat in with his [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) for the turn. In the mirror, there were various cards of particular importance, and Bland landed one of them. [Gideon Jura](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Gideon+Jura) quickly went up to 8 loyalty to force attacks from Jackson to which he happily obliged, knocking Jura down to 1.

Bland emptied his hand casting [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), before again upping Jura's loyalty to force attacks. With Baneslayer held back as a blocker, attacks could easily be a messy proposition for Jackson.

Jackson cast a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and a [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), tapping his Hierarch for mana so it wouldn't have to attack, and then getting stuck in with his knight that was big enough to eat [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel), thanks to exalted. Bland used Jura to kill off one [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary), but the second one avenged it thanks to [Sejiri Steppe](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sejiri+Steppe), which stopped Bland from being able to block it.

First [Linvala, Keeper of Silence](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Linvala%2C+Keeper+of+Silence) came down for Jackson, and then a [Basilisk Collar](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Basilisk+Collar) for it. Bland, with just one card in hand, seemed resigned to not winning the game.

"I think that first of all I need to draw Linvala, and then draw pretty well following that to not just straight up be dead here."

He didn't have one though, and it was on to Game 3.

Joe Jackson 2 – 0 Richard Bland

Between games, Bland pointed out to Jackson little bits of scrappy play from the last game, not that it had mattered to the result. The fact that these two players regularly play together can only help the National Team for Chiba in December, and the tone seemed quite convivial in spite of the importance of the match.

"You have more chances to win Nationals than me, I'm an old man!" quipped Jackson.

"You're two up! Just win one more"

As Bland drew his hand, he just mumbled to himself. "Come on, a hand with a [Forest](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Forest) and an accelerant... one time!"

Bland kept his hand, and had a [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise). Jackson had the same. Bland went for it with turn two [Fauna Shaman](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Fauna+Shaman), which immediately met an [Oblivion Ring](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Oblivion+Ring).

"New plan... aggro!" Bland cast a pair of copies of [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and attacked with [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) for two.

"Similar plan!" For Jackson it was [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) and [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage).

When Bland had a [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage), he got to hit in for three with his [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise).

Linvala from Joe looked good to make Bland's life very difficult. He simply swung with a [Raging Ravine](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Raging+Ravine), which thanks to exalted was a 7/7.

A [Sun Titan](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sun+Titan) from Joe got back a [Qasali Pridemage](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Qasali+Pridemage), meaning his Linvala could swing in for five. Bland passed without plays. Linvala then swung Bland down to 9, and followed up with [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel). Bland played a [Knight of the Reliquary](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Knight+of+the+Reliquary) and passed. He sighed a little as he saw [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) scoring a Knight for Jackson, and then a little more when he lost [Birds of Paradise](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Birds+of+Paradise) to [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel).

"I'm an old man Richard, my heart can't stand up so well" remarked Joe as he tried to get a read on if Richard had drawn some sort of answer to his overwhelming board position.

A [Bloodbraid Elf](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Bloodbraid+Elf) fetched out a [Noble Hierarch](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Noble+Hierarch) that pumped [Baneslayer Angel](https://web.archive.org/web/20220526091448/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Baneslayer+Angel)'s power to just one point shy of Bland's life total. He attacked his friend down to one life.

Bland drew his card and extended his hand, he had no more fight in him.

Joe Jackson wins 3 – 0

Congratulations to Joe Jackson, the winner of Great Britain Nationals 2010!

![](https://i.ormos.uk/fd/94/fd94f1e159af8d2d6a4bc146dd737e23e29bf60237e99e63913480f1dbfa18a3.jpg)
